# Ministerial and authority orders (ordine): 2019

1128 acts, 1,128 versions. Each act lives in a folder named by its official identifier, `<kind>/<year>/<number>/` (Legea nr. 31/1990 is `lege/1990/31/`); an order adds its issuer, `ordin/<issuer>/<year>/<number>[-<series>]/` (`ordin/justitie/2012/492-c/`); its README.md lists its articles and the decisions made by hand. Dates are in-force days. Generated on every publish; the same list is in [2019.json](2019.json).

## Ministerial and authority orders (ordine) (1128, of which 1 amending acts)
